 界面参考 jwt.io 的解码器布局：左侧 Encoded Token，右侧 Decoded Header / Payload。输入后自动解析，JWT 三段分别着色；右侧支持 JSON 高亮与中文字段解释切换，以及点击复制 Header / Payload。复制内容会进入系统剪贴板，清空页面不会清除系统剪贴板。摘要与诊断位于输入区下方的折叠面板。
 
+## 用途入口
+
+- 提供 Access Token / ID Token / Refresh Token 三个标签页，默认 Access Token；可点击或使用左右方向键、Home / End 切换。
+- Access 与 ID 复用现有 JWT/JWE/不透明格式处理、中文字段解释与验签能力。ID 入口不新增 nonce、预期受众等用途专属校验；平台字段说明仍是通用知识，不代表适用于所有 ID Token。
+- Refresh 只展示输入格式与限制提示，不展示解码字段或复制结果、不验签、不下载公钥、不执行刷新或换票请求。
+- 入口是用户选择的用途，不是工具自动识别或确认的用途；仅凭字符串格式不能确定 Access / ID / Refresh。
+- 切换到另一入口即清空 Token、手动密钥、结果及内存公钥缓存，取消待执行解析与验签。不会分别保存各入口凭据，不改变已有手动算法选择策略。
+
 ## 自动验签
 
 - 默认自动模式：全球云 Entra v1/v2 的 RS256 JWT 根据精确发行方格式和 `kid` 自动下载微软 JWKS，并在本地验签。仅 GET `https://login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant}/discovery/keys` 或 `.../discovery/v2.0/keys`；不携带 Token、Authorization、Cookie 或 Referrer，不跟随重定向。
 - 此模式需要联网。微软可见本机 IP 和公开发行方租户 ID。网络/CORS/超时或缺少匹配公钥时显示“未验证”，不伪报“签名不匹配”。其他发行方和主权云请使用手动模式，不访问 Token 内的 `jku`、`x5u` 或任意发行方地址。
 - 手动密钥模式完全离线：输入可信 SPKI PEM 公钥、X.509 证书或公开 JWK/JWKS；不接受私钥。也支持 HS256/384/512 原文或 Base64 共享密钥，按算法要求至少 32/48/64 字节。算法需独立选择，不能由 Token 自动降低验签策略。
 - 支持 RS256/384/512、PS256/384/512、ES256/384/512、EdDSA 与 HS256/384/512。浏览器须支持所选 Web Crypto 算法。
-- 输入 Token、密钥、算法变化后自动验签；无须点击验证按钮。清空时移除 Token、密钥及内存公钥缓存。异步旧结果不会覆盖新输入。
+- Access / ID 入口输入 Token、密钥、算法变化后自动验签；无须点击验证按钮。清空或切换入口时移除 Token、密钥及内存公钥缓存。异步旧结果不会覆盖新输入；Refresh 入口禁用验签。
 - 仅做签名/MAC 匹配，不等于完整 Token 有效性验证：过期时间单独显示，不校验预期发行方/受众、撤销或 API 权限。手动密钥来源、证书信任链需自行确认。自动模式从已知微软来源取公钥，但不将其等同于完整租户授权验证。
 - 公钥仅在页面内存缓存 5 分钟，最多 8 个发行方。若密钥轮换造成找不到 `kid`，清空页面后重试可重新取公钥。不在磁盘存储 Token、共享密钥或公钥缓存。
 
@@ -42,7 +50,7 @@ npm run preview
 - 紧凑 JWE 公开 Header 展示，不解密、不验证加密参数或完整性。
 - 不透明或其他未知 Token 提示限制。Entra Refresh Token 只能由 Microsoft identity platform 读取。
 - 使用本机时钟展示 UTC 时间与声明时间诊断；缺失 exp 不代表永不过期。
-- 不能仅凭字符串确定 Access / Refresh / ID Token 用途。不支持 Token 刷新、实际 API 调用或服务端撤销检查。
+- 不能仅凭字符串确定 Access / Refresh / ID Token 用途；用途标签页不改变这一限制。不支持 Token 刷新、实际 API 调用或服务端撤销检查。
 
 不上传 Token、不自动读取剪贴板、不记录凭据、不使用 localStorage/sessionStorage、无历史记录或外部脚本。自动验签仅下载公开微软密钥；切换手动模式可完全离线。清空按钮移除页面输入与结果，但不承诺擦除浏览器进程内存；请使用可信本机与浏览器，警惕浏览器扩展和共享屏幕。

 - `src/core.ts`：格式分类、jose 解码、时间诊断、统一 `InspectionResult`。
 - `src/providers.ts`：普通模块注册的厂商解释器，目前仅 Entra ID；提供全部官方声明的中文解释与常见取值匹配。
 - `src/permissions.ts`：权限值、第一方客户端 ID、受众静态知识表与匹配规则（来源含 GraphPreConsentExplorer 数据集），提供高危标记。

+- `src/modes/`：Access、ID、Refresh 的独立入口定义；Access / ID 共享底层解析，Refresh 不向展示层传递解码声明。

 - 页面及 CSS：单页响应式界面，没有后端、路由、数据库或 CDN。

+++ b/after-source/token-inspector/src/decoder.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,251 @@
+import { MAX_INPUT_BYTES, type InspectionResult } from './core';

+import { clearVerificationCache, verifySignature, type SignatureResult } from './verification';

+import { audienceNames, clientApp, permissionInfo, roleValues, splitScopes } from './permissions';

+

+export function createDecoder() {

+  const $ = <T extends HTMLElement>(id: string) => document.getElementById(id) as T;

+  const input = $<HTMLTextAreaElement>('token');

+  const parts = ['header', 'payload'] as const;

+  type Part = typeof parts[number];

+  let current: InspectionResult | undefined;

+  let enabled = true;

+  let revision = 0;

+  let verificationRevision = 0;

+  let verificationTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;

+  let verificationRequest: AbortController | undefined;

+  const keyInput = $<HTMLTextAreaElement>('verification-key');

+

+  function resetSignature() {

+    verificationRevision++;

+    clearTimeout(verificationTimer);

+    verificationRequest?.abort();

+    $('signature-status').textContent = '签名未验证';

+    $('signature-status').dataset.state = 'idle';

+    $('signature-badge').textContent = '签名未验证';

+    $('signature-badge').dataset.state = 'idle';

+    $('signature-source').textContent = '';

+    $('signature-time').textContent = '';

+  }

+

+  function displaySignature(result: SignatureResult) {

+    $('signature-status').textContent = result.message;

+    $('signature-status').dataset.state = result.state;

+    $('signature-badge').textContent = result.state === 'verified' ? '签名匹配' : result.state === 'invalid' ? '签名不匹配' : '签名未验证';

+    $('signature-badge').dataset.state = result.state;

+    $('signature-source').textContent = result.source ? `密钥来源：${result.source}` : '';

+  }

+

+  function scheduleVerification() {

+    resetSignature();

+    if (!enabled || !input.value.trim()) return;

+    const version = verificationRevision;

+    verificationTimer = setTimeout(async () => {

+      $('signature-status').textContent = '正在自动验签…';

+      verificationRequest = new AbortController();

+      const result = await verifySignature(input.value, {

+        mode: $<HTMLSelectElement>('key-source').value as 'entra' | 'manual',

+        algorithm: $<HTMLSelectElement>('signature-algorithm').value,

+        key: keyInput.value,

+        base64Secret: $<HTMLInputElement>('secret-base64').checked,

+        signal: verificationRequest.signal,

+      });

+      if (version !== verificationRevision) return;

+      displaySignature(result);

+      const decoded = current;

+      if (decoded?.status === 'decoded') {

+        const exp = decoded.claims?.exp, nbf = decoded.claims?.nbf;

+        const now = Date.now() / 1000;

+        $('signature-time').textContent = typeof exp === 'number' && now >= exp ? '时间声明：按 exp 已过期（签名仍可能匹配）。' : typeof nbf === 'number' && now < nbf ? '时间声明：按 nbf 尚未生效。' : decoded.times.exp ? '时间声明：按本机时钟暂未过期；不代表实际可用。' : '时间声明：缺少或存在异常 exp，无法确定过期时间。';

+      }

+    }, 200);

+  }

+

+  // Only serialized JSON is highlighted; untrusted values always use text nodes.

+  function showJson(target: HTMLElement, value: Record<string, unknown>) {

+    const json = JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);

+    const pattern = /"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"(?=\s*:)|"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|\b(?:true|false|null)\b|-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:[eE][+-]?\d+)?/g;

+    let offset = 0;

+    target.replaceChildren();

+    for (const match of json.matchAll(pattern)) {

+      target.append(document.createTextNode(json.slice(offset, match.index)));

+      const span = document.createElement('span');

+      const text = match[0];

+      span.className = text.startsWith('"') ? (/^\s*:/.test(json.slice(match.index! + text.length)) ? 'json-key' : 'json-string') : 'json-literal';

+      span.textContent = text;

+      target.append(span);

+      offset = match.index! + text.length;

+    }

+    target.append(document.createTextNode(json.slice(offset)));

+  }

+  function switchView(part: Part, view: string) {

+    $(`${part}-json`).hidden = view !== 'json';

+    $(`${part}-claims`).hidden = view !== 'claims';

+    for (const button of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>(`[data-part="${part}"]`)) {

+      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(button.dataset.view === view));

+    }

+  }

+  function clearResult(message = '等待输入') {

+    resetSignature();

+    revision++;

+    current = undefined;

+    $('diagnostics').hidden = true;

+    for (const id of ['summary', 'warnings', 'header-fields', 'payload-fields', 'copy-status']) $(id).replaceChildren();

+    for (const part of parts) {

+      $(`${part}-json`).textContent = message;

+      $<HTMLButtonElement>(`copy-${part}`).disabled = true;

+      switchView(part, 'json');

+    }

+    $('status').textContent = message;

+  }

+  function colorToken() {

+    const mirror = $('token-colors');

+    mirror.style.width = `${input.clientWidth}px`;

+    mirror.replaceChildren();

+    const raw = input.value;

+    const prefix = raw.match(/^\s*(?:Bearer\s+)?/i)?.[0] ?? '';

+    const segments = raw.slice(prefix.length).split('.');

+    if (segments.length === 3 && raw.length <= MAX_INPUT_BYTES) {

+      mirror.append(document.createTextNode(prefix));

+      segments.forEach((segment, index) => {

+        if (index) mirror.append(document.createTextNode('.'));

+        const span = document.createElement('span');

+        span.className = `token-segment ${['header', 'payload', 'signature'][index]}-color`;

+        span.textContent = segment;

+        mirror.append(span);

+      });

+    } else mirror.textContent = raw;

+    mirror.append(document.createTextNode('\u200b'));

+    mirror.scrollTop = input.scrollTop;

+    mirror.scrollLeft = input.scrollLeft;

+  }

+  function row(label: string, value: string) {

+    const item = document.createElement('div'); item.className = 'summary-row';

+    const key = document.createElement('span'); key.textContent = label;

+    const text = document.createElement('strong'); text.textContent = value;

+    item.append(key, text); $('summary').append(item);

+  }

+  function permissionChips(values: string[]): HTMLElement {

+    const list = document.createElement('div');

+    list.className = 'perm-list';

+    for (const value of values) {

+      const info = permissionInfo(value);

+      const chip = document.createElement('span');

+      chip.className = 'perm-chip' + (info?.critical ? ' perm-critical' : '');

+      chip.textContent = value;

+      chip.title = info ? `${info.description}${info.critical ? ' · 重要（标红）' : ''}` : '未收录的权限值';

+      list.append(chip);

+    }

+    return list;

+  }

+  function matchNote(label: string, critical: boolean): HTMLElement {

+    const note = document.createElement('span');

+    note.className = 'value-match' + (critical ? ' value-critical' : '');

+    note.textContent = `${label}${critical ? '（重要）' : ''}`;

+    return note;

+  }

+  function render(result: InspectionResult) {

+    current = result;

+    $('diagnostics').hidden = false;

+    $('status').textContent = !enabled && result.status !== 'error' ? `格式：${result.format} · 不展示声明` : { decoded: '格式有效 · 已解码', encrypted: '已识别 JWE · 载荷加密', opaque: '不透明或未知格式 · 无法读取内容', error: '未能解析 · 请检查输入' }[result.status];

+    row('格式', result.format);

+    if (enabled) row('发行方线索', result.provider);

+    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(result.times)) row(`${key} · UTC`, value);

+    for (const message of [...result.evidence, ...result.warnings]) {

+      const li = document.createElement('li'); li.textContent = message; $('warnings').append(li);

+    }

+    for (const part of parts) {

+      const value = part === 'header' ? result.header : result.claims;

+      if (value) {

+        showJson($(`${part}-json`), value);

+        $<HTMLButtonElement>(`copy-${part}`).disabled = false;

+      } else {

+        $(`${part}-json`).textContent = part === 'payload' && result.status === 'encrypted' ? '载荷已加密，无法显示。' : '没有可解码的内容。';

+      }

+      for (const field of result.fields.filter(field => field.name.startsWith(`${part}.`))) {

+        const fieldName = field.name.slice(part.length + 1);

+        const tr = document.createElement('tr');

+        const td = document.createElement('td');

+        const key = document.createElement('strong'); key.textContent = fieldName;

+        td.append(key);

+        const scopeValues = part === 'payload' && fieldName === 'scp' ? splitScopes(field.value) : [];

+        const roleList = part === 'payload' && fieldName === 'roles' ? roleValues(field.value) : [];

+        if (scopeValues.length || roleList.length) {

+          td.append(permissionChips(scopeValues.length ? scopeValues : roleList));

+        } else {

+          const value = document.createElement('code'); value.textContent = JSON.stringify(field.value, null, 2);

+          td.append(value);

+        }

+        if (part === 'payload' && (fieldName === 'azp' || fieldName === 'appid') && typeof field.value === 'string') {

+          const app = clientApp(field.value);

+          if (app) {

+            const note = matchNote(`应用：${app.name}${app.note ? `（${app.note}）` : ''}`, Boolean(app.critical));

+            if (app.foci) note.title = 'FOCI 客户端：刷新 Token 可在同一家族客户端间换取新 Token';

+            else if (app.broci) note.title = 'BroCi 客户端：可经门户经纪端点换取其他客户端的 Token';

+            td.append(note);

+          }

+        }

+        if (part === 'payload' && fieldName === 'aud') {

+          for (const name of audienceNames(field.value)) td.append(matchNote(`API：${name}`, false));

+        }

+        const explanation = document.createElement('td'); explanation.textContent = field.description;

+        const matched = part === 'payload' && (scopeValues.length || roleList.length)

+          ? [...scopeValues, ...roleList].map(value => [value, permissionInfo(value)] as const).filter(([, info]) => info?.description)

+          : [];

+        if (matched.length) explanation.append(document.createTextNode(`值匹配：${matched.map(([value, info]) => `${value}=${info!.description}`).join('；')}`));

+        if (part === 'payload' && Object.hasOwn(result.times, fieldName)) explanation.append(document.createTextNode(` UTC：${result.times[fieldName]}`));

+        tr.append(td, explanation); $(`${part}-fields`).append(tr);

+      }

+    }

+    scheduleVerification();

+  }

+  input.addEventListener('scroll', () => {

+    $('token-colors').scrollTop = input.scrollTop;

+    $('token-colors').scrollLeft = input.scrollLeft;

+  });

+  window.addEventListener('resize', colorToken);

+  function clearAll() {

+    input.value = '';

+    keyInput.value = '';

+    clearVerificationCache();

+    $('token-colors').replaceChildren();

+    $('byte-count').textContent = '0 B';

+    clearResult();

+  }

+  function updateKeyControls() {

+    const manual = $<HTMLSelectElement>('key-source').value === 'manual';

+    $('manual-key').hidden = !manual;

+    $('network-notice').hidden = manual;

+    const hmac = $<HTMLSelectElement>('signature-algorithm').value.startsWith('HS');

+    $('secret-options').hidden = !hmac;

+    $('key-label').textContent = hmac ? '共享密钥（原文逐字使用；不自动裁剪空白）' : '公钥：PEM / 证书 / JWK / JWKS（不要输入私钥）';

+    keyInput.placeholder = hmac ? '输入共享密钥后自动验签' : '粘贴可信公钥，输入后自动验签';

+    scheduleVerification();

+  }

+  $('key-source').addEventListener('change', () => { clearVerificationCache(); updateKeyControls(); });

+  $('signature-algorithm').addEventListener('change', updateKeyControls);

+  $('secret-base64').addEventListener('change', scheduleVerification);

+  keyInput.addEventListener('input', scheduleVerification);

+  for (const button of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-view]')) {

+    button.addEventListener('click', () => switchView(button.dataset.part as Part, button.dataset.view!));

+  }

+  for (const part of parts) {

+    $(`copy-${part}`).addEventListener('click', async () => {

+      const value = part === 'header' ? current?.header : current?.claims;

+      if (!value) return;

+      const version = revision;

+      try {

+        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(value, null, 2));

+        if (version === revision) $('copy-status').textContent = `${part === 'header' ? 'Header' : 'Payload'} 已复制到剪贴板。`;

+      } catch {

+        if (version === revision) $('copy-status').textContent = '复制失败，请手动选择 JSON 复制。';

+      }

+    });

+  }

+  return {

+    render,

+    reset: clearResult,

+    clear: clearAll,

+    colorToken,

+    setEnabled(value: boolean) { enabled = value; resetSignature(); },

+  };

+}

+import { inspectToken } from '../core';
+import type { TokenMode } from './types';
+
+export const idMode: TokenMode = {
+  label: 'ID Token',
+  description: '用于向客户端传递认证信息。复用通用字段说明与验签，不执行 ID Token 专属校验。',
+  canDecode: true,
+  inspect: inspectToken,
+};

+import { inspectToken, type InspectionResult } from '../core';

+import type { TokenMode } from './types';

+

+function inspectRefresh(input: string): InspectionResult {

+  const result = inspectToken(input);

+  // Reuse format validation, but never pass decoded credentials to the view.

+  return {

+    format: result.format,

+    status: result.status,

+    verification: 'not-performed',

+    provider: '未知 / 未识别',

+    evidence: [],

+    fields: [],

+    times: {},

+    warnings: result.status === 'error' ? result.warnings.slice(1) : [

+      '仅识别输入格式，不展示声明、不解密、不验签，也不执行刷新或换票请求。',

+      'Entra Refresh Token 只能由 Microsoft identity platform 读取；格式不能确认用途或实际可用性。',

+    ],

+  };

+}

+

+export const refreshMode: TokenMode = {

+  label: 'Refresh Token',

+  description: '用于请求新 Token 的凭据。此入口只识别格式，不执行刷新、换票、解密或验签。',

+  canDecode: false,

+  inspect: inspectRefresh,

+};

+import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
+import { base64url, exportJWK, generateKeyPair, SignJWT } from 'jose';
+
+const jwt = (claims: unknown = { sub: 'synthetic-purpose-user', exp: 1 }) =>
+  `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"RS256"}')}.${base64url.encode(JSON.stringify(claims))}.AA`;
+
+test('defaults to Access and supports accessible keyboard navigation without storage', async ({ page }) => {
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await expect(page.getByRole('tab')).toHaveCount(3);
+  const access = page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Access Token', exact: true });
+  await expect(access).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
+  await expect(page.getByRole('tabpanel')).toHaveAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'tab-access');
+  await access.focus();
+  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowRight');
+  await expect(page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'ID Token', exact: true })).toBeFocused();
+  await expect(page.locator('#purpose-title')).toHaveText('ID Token');
+  await expect(page.getByRole('tabpanel')).toHaveAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'tab-id');
+  await expect(page.locator('#details')).toBeVisible();
+  await page.keyboard.press('End');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-details')).toBeVisible();
+  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowRight');
+  await expect(access).toBeFocused();
+  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowLeft');
+  await expect(page.locator('#tab-refresh')).toBeFocused();
+  await page.keyboard.press('Home');
+  await expect(access).toHaveAttribute('tabindex', '0');
+  expect(await page.evaluate(() => [localStorage.length, sessionStorage.length])).toEqual([0, 0]);
+});
+
+test('ID retains manual signature validation and switching clears credentials and results', async ({ page }) => {
+  const secret = 'purpose-fixture-secret-at-least-32-bytes';
+  const token = await new SignJWT({ sub: 'synthetic-id-user' }).setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'HS256' }).sign(new TextEncoder().encode(secret));
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#tab-id').click();
+  await page.locator('#key-source').selectOption('manual');
+  await page.locator('#signature-algorithm').selectOption('HS256');
+  await page.locator('#verification-key').fill(secret);
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(token);
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toContainText('Signature Verified');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).toContainText('synthetic-id-user');
+  await page.locator('#tab-access').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#verification-key')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#copy-payload')).toBeDisabled();
+  await expect(page.locator('#byte-count')).toHaveText('0 B');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toHaveText('签名未验证');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-source')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-time')).toHaveText('');
+  await page.locator('#tab-id').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-algorithm')).toHaveValue('HS256');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('opaque-example');
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('不透明');
+  const jwe = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"dir","enc":"A256GCM"}')}..AA.AA.AA`;
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(jwe);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('JWE');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).toContainText('载荷已加密');
+});
+
+test('Refresh exposes formats and limits, never claims, copying or public-key requests', async ({ page }) => {
+  const requests: string[] = [];
+  await page.route('https://**/*', route => { requests.push(route.request().url()); return route.abort(); });
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  const entra = jwt({ iss: 'https://sts.windows.net/11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111/', sub: '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>', scp: 'User.Read', exp: 1 });
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(entra);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('格式：JWT');
+  await expect(page.locator('#details')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#copy-header')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#copy-payload')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-badge')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#header-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).not.toContainText('User.Read');
+  await expect(page.locator('#summary')).not.toContainText('exp');
+  const jwe = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"dir","enc":"A256GCM"}')}..AA.AA.AA`;
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(jwe);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('格式：JWE');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('opaque-example');
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('Opaque / Unknown');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('abc.def.ghi');
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('未能解析');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('x'.repeat(65537));
+  await expect(page.locator('#warnings')).toContainText('64 KiB');
+  await page.waitForTimeout(400);
+  expect(requests).toEqual([]);
+  await page.screenshot({ path: 'test-results/refresh-desktop.png', fullPage: true });
+  await page.locator('#clear').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#diagnostics')).toBeHidden();
+});
+
+test('switching cancels pending parsing and clears JWKS cache after automatic ID verification', async ({ page }) => {
+  const pair = await generateKeyPair('RS256');
+  const key = { ...await exportJWK(pair.publicKey), kid: 'purpose-test', alg: 'RS256', use: 'sig' };
+  const token = await new SignJWT({ sub: 'synthetic-purpose-user' })
+    .setIssuer('https://sts.windows.net/11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111/')
+    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'RS256', kid: key.kid }).sign(pair.privateKey);
+  let requests = 0;
+  await page.route('https://login.microsoftonline.com/**', route => {
+    requests++;
+    return route.fulfill({ json: { keys: [key] }, headers: { 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } });
+  });
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.evaluate(value => {
+    const input = document.getElementById('token') as HTMLTextAreaElement;
+    input.value = value;
+    input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
+    document.getElementById('tab-id')!.click();
+  }, token);
+  await page.waitForTimeout(450);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toHaveText('等待输入');
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  expect(requests).toBe(0);
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(token);
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toContainText('Signature Verified');
+  expect(requests).toBe(1);
+  await page.locator('#tab-access').click();
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(token);
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toContainText('Signature Verified');
+  expect(requests).toBe(2);
+});
+
+test('switching during a key request blocks late results in Refresh and the next decoder', async ({ page }) => {
+  const pair = await generateKeyPair('RS256');
+  const key = { ...await exportJWK(pair.publicKey), kid: 'delayed-purpose-key', alg: 'RS256' };
+  const token = await new SignJWT({ sub: 'late-user' })
+    .setIssuer('https://sts.windows.net/11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111/')
+    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'RS256', kid: key.kid }).sign(pair.privateKey);
+  let release: () => void = () => {};
+  const released = new Promise<void>(resolve => { release = resolve; });
+  let requested = false;
+  await page.route('https://login.microsoftonline.com/**', async route => {
+    requested = true;
+    await released;
+    await route.fulfill({ json: { keys: [key] }, headers: { 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } }).catch(() => {});
+  });
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(token);
+  await expect.poll(() => requested).toBe(true);
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  release();
+  await page.waitForTimeout(450);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toHaveText('等待输入');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toHaveText('签名未验证');
+  await page.locator('#tab-id').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).not.toContainText('late-user');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toHaveText('签名未验证');
+});
+

+  }
+  await page.screenshot({ path: 'test-results/refresh-mobile.png', fullPage: true });

+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

+import { base64url } from 'jose';

+import { inspectToken, MAX_INPUT_BYTES } from '../src/core';

+import { modes } from '../src/modes';

+

+const jwt = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"RS256"}')}.${base64url.encode('{"sub":"synthetic-secret","exp":1}')}.AA`;

+const jwe = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"dir","enc":"A256GCM"}')}..AA.AA.AA`;

+

+describe('purpose entry boundaries', () => {

+  for (const purpose of ['access', 'id'] as const) {

+    for (const input of [jwt, jwe, 'opaque-example', 'abc.def.ghi']) {

+      it(`${purpose} keeps existing inspection for ${input === jwt ? 'JWT' : input === jwe ? 'JWE' : input}`, () => {

+        expect(modes[purpose].canDecode).toBe(true);

+        expect(modes[purpose].inspect(input)).toEqual(inspectToken(input));

+      });

+    }

+  }

+

+  it.each([[jwt, 'JWT'], [jwe, 'JWE'], ['opaque-example', 'Opaque / Unknown']])('refresh only exposes format for %s', (input, format) => {

+    const result = modes.refresh.inspect(input);

+    expect(modes.refresh.canDecode).toBe(false);

+    expect(result.format).toBe(format);

+    expect(result.header).toBeUndefined();

+    expect(result.claims).toBeUndefined();

+    expect(result.fields).toEqual([]);

+    expect(result.times).toEqual({});

+    expect(result.evidence).toEqual([]);

+    expect(result.verification).toBe('not-performed');

+    expect(JSON.stringify(result)).not.toContain('synthetic-secret');

+  });

+

+  it.each([

+    ['empty', ''], ['bare Bearer', 'Bearer '], ['whitespace', 'a b'],

+    ['malformed JWT', 'abc.def.ghi'], ['oversize', 'x'.repeat(MAX_INPUT_BYTES + 1)],

+  ])('refresh retains validation errors: %s', (_label, input) => {

+    const result = modes.refresh.inspect(input);

+    expect(result.status).toBe('error');

+    expect(result.warnings.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

+    expect(result.claims).toBeUndefined();

+  });

+});

 界面参考 jwt.io 的解码器布局：左侧 Encoded Token，右侧 Decoded Header / Payload。输入后自动解析，JWT 三段分别着色；右侧支持 JSON 高亮与中文字段解释切换，以及点击复制 Header / Payload。复制内容会进入系统剪贴板，清空页面不会清除系统剪贴板。摘要与诊断位于输入区下方的折叠面板。
 
+## 用途入口
+
+- 提供 Access Token / ID Token / Refresh Token 三个标签页，默认 Access Token；可点击或使用左右方向键、Home / End 切换。
+- Access 与 ID 复用现有 JWT/JWE/不透明格式处理、中文字段解释与验签能力。ID 入口不新增 nonce、预期受众等用途专属校验；平台字段说明仍是通用知识，不代表适用于所有 ID Token。
+- Refresh 只展示输入格式与限制提示，不展示解码字段或复制结果、不验签、不下载公钥、不执行刷新或换票请求。
+- 入口是用户选择的用途，不是工具自动识别或确认的用途；仅凭字符串格式不能确定 Access / ID / Refresh。
+- 切换到另一入口即清空 Token、手动密钥、结果及内存公钥缓存，取消待执行解析与验签。不会分别保存各入口凭据，不改变已有手动算法选择策略。
+
 ## 自动验签
 
 - 默认自动模式：全球云 Entra v1/v2 的 RS256 JWT 根据精确发行方格式和 `kid` 自动下载微软 JWKS，并在本地验签。仅 GET `https://login.microsoftonline.com/{tenant}/discovery/keys` 或 `.../discovery/v2.0/keys`；不携带 Token、Authorization、Cookie 或 Referrer，不跟随重定向。
 - 此模式需要联网。微软可见本机 IP 和公开发行方租户 ID。网络/CORS/超时或缺少匹配公钥时显示“未验证”，不伪报“签名不匹配”。其他发行方和主权云请使用手动模式，不访问 Token 内的 `jku`、`x5u` 或任意发行方地址。
 - 手动密钥模式完全离线：输入可信 SPKI PEM 公钥、X.509 证书或公开 JWK/JWKS；不接受私钥。也支持 HS256/384/512 原文或 Base64 共享密钥，按算法要求至少 32/48/64 字节。算法需独立选择，不能由 Token 自动降低验签策略。
 - 支持 RS256/384/512、PS256/384/512、ES256/384/512、EdDSA 与 HS256/384/512。浏览器须支持所选 Web Crypto 算法。
-- 输入 Token、密钥、算法变化后自动验签；无须点击验证按钮。清空时移除 Token、密钥及内存公钥缓存。异步旧结果不会覆盖新输入。
+- Access / ID 入口输入 Token、密钥、算法变化后自动验签；无须点击验证按钮。清空或切换入口时移除 Token、密钥及内存公钥缓存。异步旧结果不会覆盖新输入；Refresh 入口禁用验签。
 - 仅做签名/MAC 匹配，不等于完整 Token 有效性验证：过期时间单独显示，不校验预期发行方/受众、撤销或 API 权限。手动密钥来源、证书信任链需自行确认。自动模式从已知微软来源取公钥，但不将其等同于完整租户授权验证。
 - 公钥仅在页面内存缓存 5 分钟，最多 8 个发行方。若密钥轮换造成找不到 `kid`，清空页面后重试可重新取公钥。不在磁盘存储 Token、共享密钥或公钥缓存。
 
@@ -42,7 +50,7 @@ npm run preview
 - 紧凑 JWE 公开 Header 展示，不解密、不验证加密参数或完整性。
 - 不透明或其他未知 Token 提示限制。Entra Refresh Token 只能由 Microsoft identity platform 读取。
 - 使用本机时钟展示 UTC 时间与声明时间诊断；缺失 exp 不代表永不过期。
-- 不能仅凭字符串确定 Access / Refresh / ID Token 用途。不支持 Token 刷新、实际 API 调用或服务端撤销检查。
+- 不能仅凭字符串确定 Access / Refresh / ID Token 用途；用途标签页不改变这一限制。不支持 Token 刷新、实际 API 调用或服务端撤销检查。
 
 不上传 Token、不自动读取剪贴板、不记录凭据、不使用 localStorage/sessionStorage、无历史记录或外部脚本。自动验签仅下载公开微软密钥；切换手动模式可完全离线。清空按钮移除页面输入与结果，但不承诺擦除浏览器进程内存；请使用可信本机与浏览器，警惕浏览器扩展和共享屏幕。

 - `src/core.ts`：格式分类、jose 解码、时间诊断、统一 `InspectionResult`。
 - `src/providers.ts`：普通模块注册的厂商解释器，目前仅 Entra ID；提供全部官方声明的中文解释与常见取值匹配。
 - `src/permissions.ts`：权限值、第一方客户端 ID、受众静态知识表与匹配规则（来源含 GraphPreConsentExplorer 数据集），提供高危标记。
-- `src/main.ts`：页面交互；所有不可信字段通过 textContent 渲染。

+- `src/modes/`：Access、ID、Refresh 的独立入口定义；Access / ID 共享底层解析，Refresh 不向展示层传递解码声明。
+- `src/decoder.ts`：共享的解码展示、复制与验签交互；所有不可信字段通过 textContent 渲染，Refresh 模式阻止验签调度。
 - `src/verification.ts`：基于 jose/Web Crypto 的独立验签、可信微软公钥下载、输入策略与内存缓存。
 - 页面及 CSS：单页响应式界面，没有后端、路由、数据库或 CDN。

+import { MAX_INPUT_BYTES, type InspectionResult } from './core';

+import { clearVerificationCache, verifySignature, type SignatureResult } from './verification';

+import { audienceNames, clientApp, permissionInfo, roleValues, splitScopes } from './permissions';

+

+export function createDecoder() {

+  const $ = <T extends HTMLElement>(id: string) => document.getElementById(id) as T;

+  const input = $<HTMLTextAreaElement>('token');

+  const parts = ['header', 'payload'] as const;

+  type Part = typeof parts[number];

+  let current: InspectionResult | undefined;

+  let enabled = true;

+  let revision = 0;

+  let verificationRevision = 0;

+  let verificationTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;

+  let verificationRequest: AbortController | undefined;

+  const keyInput = $<HTMLTextAreaElement>('verification-key');

+

+  function resetSignature() {

+    verificationRevision++;

+    clearTimeout(verificationTimer);

+    verificationRequest?.abort();

+    $('signature-status').textContent = '签名未验证';

+    $('signature-status').dataset.state = 'idle';

+    $('signature-badge').textContent = '签名未验证';

+    $('signature-badge').dataset.state = 'idle';

+    $('signature-source').textContent = '';

+    $('signature-time').textContent = '';

+  }

+

+  function displaySignature(result: SignatureResult) {

+    $('signature-status').textContent = result.message;

+    $('signature-status').dataset.state = result.state;

+    $('signature-badge').textContent = result.state === 'verified' ? '签名匹配' : result.state === 'invalid' ? '签名不匹配' : '签名未验证';

+    $('signature-badge').dataset.state = result.state;

+    $('signature-source').textContent = result.source ? `密钥来源：${result.source}` : '';

+  }

+

+  function scheduleVerification() {

+    resetSignature();

+    if (!enabled || !input.value.trim()) return;

+    const version = verificationRevision;

+    verificationTimer = setTimeout(async () => {

+      $('signature-status').textContent = '正在自动验签…';

+      verificationRequest = new AbortController();

+      const result = await verifySignature(input.value, {

+        mode: $<HTMLSelectElement>('key-source').value as 'entra' | 'manual',

+        algorithm: $<HTMLSelectElement>('signature-algorithm').value,

+        key: keyInput.value,

+        base64Secret: $<HTMLInputElement>('secret-base64').checked,

+        signal: verificationRequest.signal,

+      });

+      if (version !== verificationRevision) return;

+      displaySignature(result);

+      const decoded = current;

+      if (decoded?.status === 'decoded') {

+        const exp = decoded.claims?.exp, nbf = decoded.claims?.nbf;

+        const now = Date.now() / 1000;

+        $('signature-time').textContent = typeof exp === 'number' && now >= exp ? '时间声明：按 exp 已过期（签名仍可能匹配）。' : typeof nbf === 'number' && now < nbf ? '时间声明：按 nbf 尚未生效。' : decoded.times.exp ? '时间声明：按本机时钟暂未过期；不代表实际可用。' : '时间声明：缺少或存在异常 exp，无法确定过期时间。';

+      }

+    }, 200);

+  }

+

+  // Only serialized JSON is highlighted; untrusted values always use text nodes.

+  function showJson(target: HTMLElement, value: Record<string, unknown>) {

+    const json = JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);

+    const pattern = /"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"(?=\s*:)|"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|\b(?:true|false|null)\b|-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:[eE][+-]?\d+)?/g;

+    let offset = 0;

+    target.replaceChildren();

+    for (const match of json.matchAll(pattern)) {

+      target.append(document.createTextNode(json.slice(offset, match.index)));

+      const span = document.createElement('span');

+      const text = match[0];

+      span.className = text.startsWith('"') ? (/^\s*:/.test(json.slice(match.index! + text.length)) ? 'json-key' : 'json-string') : 'json-literal';

+      span.textContent = text;

+      target.append(span);

+      offset = match.index! + text.length;

+    }

+    target.append(document.createTextNode(json.slice(offset)));

+  }

+  function switchView(part: Part, view: string) {

+    $(`${part}-json`).hidden = view !== 'json';

+    $(`${part}-claims`).hidden = view !== 'claims';

+    for (const button of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>(`[data-part="${part}"]`)) {

+      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(button.dataset.view === view));

+    }

+  }

+  function clearResult(message = '等待输入') {

+    resetSignature();

+    revision++;

+    current = undefined;

+    $('diagnostics').hidden = true;

+    for (const id of ['summary', 'warnings', 'header-fields', 'payload-fields', 'copy-status']) $(id).replaceChildren();

+    for (const part of parts) {

+      $(`${part}-json`).textContent = message;

+      $<HTMLButtonElement>(`copy-${part}`).disabled = true;

+      switchView(part, 'json');

+    }

+    $('status').textContent = message;

+  }

+  function colorToken() {

+    const mirror = $('token-colors');

+    mirror.style.width = `${input.clientWidth}px`;

+    mirror.replaceChildren();

+    const raw = input.value;

+    const prefix = raw.match(/^\s*(?:Bearer\s+)?/i)?.[0] ?? '';

+    const segments = raw.slice(prefix.length).split('.');

+    if (segments.length === 3 && raw.length <= MAX_INPUT_BYTES) {

+      mirror.append(document.createTextNode(prefix));

+      segments.forEach((segment, index) => {

+        if (index) mirror.append(document.createTextNode('.'));

+        const span = document.createElement('span');

+        span.className = `token-segment ${['header', 'payload', 'signature'][index]}-color`;

+        span.textContent = segment;

+        mirror.append(span);

+      });

+    } else mirror.textContent = raw;

+    mirror.append(document.createTextNode('\u200b'));

+    mirror.scrollTop = input.scrollTop;

+    mirror.scrollLeft = input.scrollLeft;

+  }

+  function row(label: string, value: string) {

+    const item = document.createElement('div'); item.className = 'summary-row';

+    const key = document.createElement('span'); key.textContent = label;

+    const text = document.createElement('strong'); text.textContent = value;

+    item.append(key, text); $('summary').append(item);

+  }

+  function permissionChips(values: string[]): HTMLElement {

+    const list = document.createElement('div');

+    list.className = 'perm-list';

+    for (const value of values) {

+      const info = permissionInfo(value);

+      const chip = document.createElement('span');

+      chip.className = 'perm-chip' + (info?.critical ? ' perm-critical' : '');

+      chip.textContent = value;

+      chip.title = info ? `${info.description}${info.critical ? ' · 重要（标红）' : ''}` : '未收录的权限值';

+      list.append(chip);

+    }

+    return list;

+  }

+  function matchNote(label: string, critical: boolean): HTMLElement {

+    const note = document.createElement('span');

+    note.className = 'value-match' + (critical ? ' value-critical' : '');

+    note.textContent = `${label}${critical ? '（重要）' : ''}`;

+    return note;

+  }

+  function render(result: InspectionResult) {

+    current = result;

+    $('diagnostics').hidden = false;

+    $('status').textContent = !enabled && result.status !== 'error' ? `格式：${result.format} · 不展示声明` : { decoded: '格式有效 · 已解码', encrypted: '已识别 JWE · 载荷加密', opaque: '不透明或未知格式 · 无法读取内容', error: '未能解析 · 请检查输入' }[result.status];

+    row('格式', result.format);

+    if (enabled) row('发行方线索', result.provider);

+    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(result.times)) row(`${key} · UTC`, value);

+    for (const message of [...result.evidence, ...result.warnings]) {

+      const li = document.createElement('li'); li.textContent = message; $('warnings').append(li);

+    }

+    for (const part of parts) {

+      const value = part === 'header' ? result.header : result.claims;

+      if (value) {

+        showJson($(`${part}-json`), value);

+        $<HTMLButtonElement>(`copy-${part}`).disabled = false;

+      } else {

+        $(`${part}-json`).textContent = part === 'payload' && result.status === 'encrypted' ? '载荷已加密，无法显示。' : '没有可解码的内容。';

+      }

+      for (const field of result.fields.filter(field => field.name.startsWith(`${part}.`))) {

+        const fieldName = field.name.slice(part.length + 1);

+        const tr = document.createElement('tr');

+        const td = document.createElement('td');

+        const key = document.createElement('strong'); key.textContent = fieldName;

+        td.append(key);

+        const scopeValues = part === 'payload' && fieldName === 'scp' ? splitScopes(field.value) : [];

+        const roleList = part === 'payload' && fieldName === 'roles' ? roleValues(field.value) : [];

+        if (scopeValues.length || roleList.length) {

+          td.append(permissionChips(scopeValues.length ? scopeValues : roleList));

+        } else {

+          const value = document.createElement('code'); value.textContent = JSON.stringify(field.value, null, 2);

+          td.append(value);

+        }

+        if (part === 'payload' && (fieldName === 'azp' || fieldName === 'appid') && typeof field.value === 'string') {

+          const app = clientApp(field.value);

+          if (app) {

+            const note = matchNote(`应用：${app.name}${app.note ? `（${app.note}）` : ''}`, Boolean(app.critical));

+            if (app.foci) note.title = 'FOCI 客户端：刷新 Token 可在同一家族客户端间换取新 Token';

+            else if (app.broci) note.title = 'BroCi 客户端：可经门户经纪端点换取其他客户端的 Token';

+            td.append(note);

+          }

+        }

+        if (part === 'payload' && fieldName === 'aud') {

+          for (const name of audienceNames(field.value)) td.append(matchNote(`API：${name}`, false));

+        }

+        const explanation = document.createElement('td'); explanation.textContent = field.description;

+        const matched = part === 'payload' && (scopeValues.length || roleList.length)

+          ? [...scopeValues, ...roleList].map(value => [value, permissionInfo(value)] as const).filter(([, info]) => info?.description)

+          : [];

+        if (matched.length) explanation.append(document.createTextNode(`值匹配：${matched.map(([value, info]) => `${value}=${info!.description}`).join('；')}`));

+        if (part === 'payload' && Object.hasOwn(result.times, fieldName)) explanation.append(document.createTextNode(` UTC：${result.times[fieldName]}`));

+        tr.append(td, explanation); $(`${part}-fields`).append(tr);

+      }

+    }

+    scheduleVerification();

+  }

+  input.addEventListener('scroll', () => {

+    $('token-colors').scrollTop = input.scrollTop;

+    $('token-colors').scrollLeft = input.scrollLeft;

+  });

+  window.addEventListener('resize', colorToken);

+  function clearAll() {

+    input.value = '';

+    keyInput.value = '';

+    clearVerificationCache();

+    $('token-colors').replaceChildren();

+    $('byte-count').textContent = '0 B';

+    clearResult();

+  }

+  function updateKeyControls() {

+    const manual = $<HTMLSelectElement>('key-source').value === 'manual';

+    $('manual-key').hidden = !manual;

+    $('network-notice').hidden = manual;

+    const hmac = $<HTMLSelectElement>('signature-algorithm').value.startsWith('HS');

+    $('secret-options').hidden = !hmac;

+    $('key-label').textContent = hmac ? '共享密钥（原文逐字使用；不自动裁剪空白）' : '公钥：PEM / 证书 / JWK / JWKS（不要输入私钥）';

+    keyInput.placeholder = hmac ? '输入共享密钥后自动验签' : '粘贴可信公钥，输入后自动验签';

+    scheduleVerification();

+  }

+  $('key-source').addEventListener('change', () => { clearVerificationCache(); updateKeyControls(); });

+  $('signature-algorithm').addEventListener('change', updateKeyControls);

+  $('secret-base64').addEventListener('change', scheduleVerification);

+  keyInput.addEventListener('input', scheduleVerification);

+  for (const button of document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-view]')) {

+    button.addEventListener('click', () => switchView(button.dataset.part as Part, button.dataset.view!));

+  }

+  for (const part of parts) {

+    $(`copy-${part}`).addEventListener('click', async () => {

+      const value = part === 'header' ? current?.header : current?.claims;

+      if (!value) return;

+      const version = revision;

+      try {

+        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(value, null, 2));

+        if (version === revision) $('copy-status').textContent = `${part === 'header' ? 'Header' : 'Payload'} 已复制到剪贴板。`;

+      } catch {

+        if (version === revision) $('copy-status').textContent = '复制失败，请手动选择 JSON 复制。';

+      }

+    });

+  }

+  return {

+    render,

+    reset: clearResult,

+    clear: clearAll,

+    colorToken,

+    setEnabled(value: boolean) { enabled = value; resetSignature(); },

+  };

+}

diff --git a/after-source/token-inspector/src/modes/refresh.ts b/after-source/token-inspector/src/modes/refresh.ts

+import { inspectToken, type InspectionResult } from '../core';

+import type { TokenMode } from './types';

+

+function inspectRefresh(input: string): InspectionResult {

+  const result = inspectToken(input);

+  // Reuse format validation, but never pass decoded credentials to the view.

+  return {

+    format: result.format,

+    status: result.status,

+    verification: 'not-performed',

+    provider: '未知 / 未识别',

+    evidence: [],

+    fields: [],

+    times: {},

+    warnings: result.status === 'error' ? result.warnings.slice(1) : [

+      '仅识别输入格式，不展示声明、不解密、不验签，也不执行刷新或换票请求。',

+      'Entra Refresh Token 只能由 Microsoft identity platform 读取；格式不能确认用途或实际可用性。',

+    ],

+  };

+}

+

+export const refreshMode: TokenMode = {

+  label: 'Refresh Token',

+  description: '用于请求新 Token 的凭据。此入口只识别格式，不执行刷新、换票、解密或验签。',

+  canDecode: false,

+  inspect: inspectRefresh,

+};

+import type { InspectionResult } from '../core';
+
+export type TokenPurpose = 'access' | 'id' | 'refresh';
+export interface TokenMode {
+  label: string;
+  description: string;
+  canDecode: boolean;
+  inspect(input: string): InspectionResult;
+}

+import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
+import { base64url, exportJWK, generateKeyPair, SignJWT } from 'jose';
+
+const jwt = (claims: unknown = { sub: 'synthetic-purpose-user', exp: 1 }) =>
+  `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"RS256"}')}.${base64url.encode(JSON.stringify(claims))}.AA`;
+
+test('defaults to Access and supports accessible keyboard navigation without storage', async ({ page }) => {
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await expect(page.getByRole('tab')).toHaveCount(3);
+  const access = page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Access Token', exact: true });
+  await expect(access).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true');
+  await expect(page.getByRole('tabpanel')).toHaveAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'tab-access');
+  await access.focus();
+  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowRight');
+  await expect(page.getByRole('tab', { name: 'ID Token', exact: true })).toBeFocused();
+  await expect(page.locator('#purpose-title')).toHaveText('ID Token');
+  await expect(page.getByRole('tabpanel')).toHaveAttribute('aria-labelledby', 'tab-id');
+  await expect(page.locator('#details')).toBeVisible();
+  await page.keyboard.press('End');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-details')).toBeVisible();
+  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowRight');
+  await expect(access).toBeFocused();
+  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowLeft');
+  await expect(page.locator('#tab-refresh')).toBeFocused();
+  await page.keyboard.press('Home');
+  await expect(access).toHaveAttribute('tabindex', '0');
+  expect(await page.evaluate(() => [localStorage.length, sessionStorage.length])).toEqual([0, 0]);
+});
+
+test('ID retains manual signature validation and switching clears credentials and results', async ({ page }) => {
+  const secret = 'purpose-fixture-secret-at-least-32-bytes';
+  const token = await new SignJWT({ sub: 'synthetic-id-user' }).setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'HS256' }).sign(new TextEncoder().encode(secret));
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#tab-id').click();
+  await page.locator('#key-source').selectOption('manual');
+  await page.locator('#signature-algorithm').selectOption('HS256');
+  await page.locator('#verification-key').fill(secret);
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(token);
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toContainText('Signature Verified');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).toContainText('synthetic-id-user');
+  await page.locator('#tab-access').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#verification-key')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#copy-payload')).toBeDisabled();
+  await expect(page.locator('#byte-count')).toHaveText('0 B');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toHaveText('签名未验证');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-source')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-time')).toHaveText('');
+  await page.locator('#tab-id').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-algorithm')).toHaveValue('HS256');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('opaque-example');
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('不透明');
+  const jwe = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"dir","enc":"A256GCM"}')}..AA.AA.AA`;
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(jwe);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('JWE');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).toContainText('载荷已加密');
+});
+
+test('Refresh exposes formats and limits, never claims, copying or public-key requests', async ({ page }) => {
+  const requests: string[] = [];
+  await page.route('https://**/*', route => { requests.push(route.request().url()); return route.abort(); });
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  const entra = jwt({ iss: 'https://sts.windows.net/11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111/', sub: '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>', scp: 'User.Read', exp: 1 });
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(entra);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('格式：JWT');
+  await expect(page.locator('#details')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#copy-header')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#copy-payload')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-badge')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#header-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).not.toContainText('User.Read');
+  await expect(page.locator('#summary')).not.toContainText('exp');
+  const jwe = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"dir","enc":"A256GCM"}')}..AA.AA.AA`;
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(jwe);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('格式：JWE');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('opaque-example');
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('Opaque / Unknown');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('abc.def.ghi');
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('未能解析');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('x'.repeat(65537));
+  await expect(page.locator('#warnings')).toContainText('64 KiB');
+  await page.waitForTimeout(400);
+  expect(requests).toEqual([]);
+  await page.screenshot({ path: 'test-results/refresh-desktop.png', fullPage: true });
+  await page.locator('#clear').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#diagnostics')).toBeHidden();
+});
+
+test('switching cancels pending parsing and clears JWKS cache after automatic ID verification', async ({ page }) => {
+  const pair = await generateKeyPair('RS256');
+  const key = { ...await exportJWK(pair.publicKey), kid: 'purpose-test', alg: 'RS256', use: 'sig' };
+  const token = await new SignJWT({ sub: 'synthetic-purpose-user' })
+    .setIssuer('https://sts.windows.net/11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111/')
+    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'RS256', kid: key.kid }).sign(pair.privateKey);
+  let requests = 0;
+  await page.route('https://login.microsoftonline.com/**', route => {
+    requests++;
+    return route.fulfill({ json: { keys: [key] }, headers: { 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } });
+  });
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.evaluate(value => {
+    const input = document.getElementById('token') as HTMLTextAreaElement;
+    input.value = value;
+    input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
+    document.getElementById('tab-id')!.click();
+  }, token);
+  await page.waitForTimeout(450);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toHaveText('等待输入');
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  expect(requests).toBe(0);
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(token);
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toContainText('Signature Verified');
+  expect(requests).toBe(1);
+  await page.locator('#tab-access').click();
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(token);
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toContainText('Signature Verified');
+  expect(requests).toBe(2);
+});
+
+test('switching during a key request blocks late results in Refresh and the next decoder', async ({ page }) => {
+  const pair = await generateKeyPair('RS256');
+  const key = { ...await exportJWK(pair.publicKey), kid: 'delayed-purpose-key', alg: 'RS256' };
+  const token = await new SignJWT({ sub: 'late-user' })
+    .setIssuer('https://sts.windows.net/11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111/')
+    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'RS256', kid: key.kid }).sign(pair.privateKey);
+  let release: () => void = () => {};
+  const released = new Promise<void>(resolve => { release = resolve; });
+  let requested = false;
+  await page.route('https://login.microsoftonline.com/**', async route => {
+    requested = true;
+    await released;
+    await route.fulfill({ json: { keys: [key] }, headers: { 'access-control-allow-origin': '*' } }).catch(() => {});
+  });
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(token);
+  await expect.poll(() => requested).toBe(true);
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  release();
+  await page.waitForTimeout(450);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toHaveText('等待输入');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toHaveText('签名未验证');
+  await page.locator('#tab-id').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).not.toContainText('late-user');
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toHaveText('签名未验证');
+});
+
+test('all purpose entries fit narrow mobile screens', async ({ page }) => {
+  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 320, height: 740 });
+  await page.goto('/');
+  for (const purpose of ['access', 'id', 'refresh']) {
+    await page.locator(`#tab-${purpose}`).click();
+    await expect(page.locator(`#tab-${purpose}`)).toBeInViewport();
+    expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth)).toBe(true);
+  }
+  await page.screenshot({ path: 'test-results/refresh-mobile.png', fullPage: true });
+});

+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

+import { base64url } from 'jose';

+import { inspectToken, MAX_INPUT_BYTES } from '../src/core';

+import { modes } from '../src/modes';

+

+const jwt = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"RS256"}')}.${base64url.encode('{"sub":"synthetic-secret","exp":1}')}.AA`;

+const jwe = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"dir","enc":"A256GCM"}')}..AA.AA.AA`;

+

+describe('purpose entry boundaries', () => {

+  for (const purpose of ['access', 'id'] as const) {

+    for (const input of [jwt, jwe, 'opaque-example', 'abc.def.ghi']) {

+      it(`${purpose} keeps existing inspection for ${input === jwt ? 'JWT' : input === jwe ? 'JWE' : input}`, () => {

+        expect(modes[purpose].canDecode).toBe(true);

+        expect(modes[purpose].inspect(input)).toEqual(inspectToken(input));

+      });

+    }

+  }

+

+  it.each([[jwt, 'JWT'], [jwe, 'JWE'], ['opaque-example', 'Opaque / Unknown']])('refresh only exposes format for %s', (input, format) => {

+    const result = modes.refresh.inspect(input);

+    expect(modes.refresh.canDecode).toBe(false);

+    expect(result.format).toBe(format);

+    expect(result.header).toBeUndefined();

+    expect(result.claims).toBeUndefined();

+    expect(result.fields).toEqual([]);

+    expect(result.times).toEqual({});

+    expect(result.evidence).toEqual([]);

+    expect(result.verification).toBe('not-performed');

+    expect(JSON.stringify(result)).not.toContain('synthetic-secret');

+  });

+

+  it.each([

+    ['empty', ''], ['bare Bearer', 'Bearer '], ['whitespace', 'a b'],

+    ['malformed JWT', 'abc.def.ghi'], ['oversize', 'x'.repeat(MAX_INPUT_BYTES + 1)],

+  ])('refresh retains validation errors: %s', (_label, input) => {

+    const result = modes.refresh.inspect(input);

+    expect(result.status).toBe('error');

+    expect(result.warnings.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

+    expect(result.claims).toBeUndefined();

+  });

+});

 - 提供 Access Token / ID Token / Refresh Token 三个标签页，默认 Access Token；可点击或使用左右方向键、Home / End 切换。
 - Access 与 ID 复用现有 JWT/JWE/不透明格式处理、中文字段解释与验签能力。ID 入口不新增 nonce、预期受众等用途专属校验；平台字段说明仍是通用知识，不代表适用于所有 ID Token。

+- Refresh 可实验性解析原始 `1.<描述段>.<不透明载荷>` 的公开描述段：仅版本 1、规范 Base64URL、解码后恰好 40 字节，按 .NET GUID 字节序提取 Tenant ID / Client ID，并匹配已知应用名。显示前缀/尾部 Hex 与不透明载荷字节数，不解释前缀/尾部语义。其他版本、长度或编码异常时停止解释，不尝试套用其他布局。

 - 入口是用户选择的用途，不是工具自动识别或确认的用途；仅凭字符串格式不能确定 Access / ID / Refresh。
 - 切换到另一入口即清空 Token、手动密钥、结果及内存公钥缓存，取消待执行解析与验签。不会分别保存各入口凭据，不改变已有手动算法选择策略。

 function inspectRefresh(input: string): InspectionResult {

+  const structure = parseRefreshStructure(input);

+  if (structure.kind !== 'unrecognized') {

+    const result: InspectionResult = {

+      format: 'Opaque / Unknown', status: 'opaque', verification: 'not-performed',

+      ],

+    };

+    if (structure.kind === 'matched') {

+      result.format = 'Entra RT / PRT（候选结构）';

+      result.refreshStructure = structure.metadata;

+    } else {

+      if (structure.kind === 'invalid') { result.format = 'Invalid'; result.status = 'error'; }

+      result.warnings.unshift(structure.message);

+    }

+    return result;

+  }

+import { base64url } from 'jose';
+import { MAX_INPUT_BYTES } from './core';
+
+export interface RefreshStructure {
+  version: '1';
+  descriptorBytes: 40;
+  prefixHex: string;
+  tenantId: string;
+  clientId: string;
+  trailerHex: string;
+  opaqueBytes: number;
+}
+
+export type RefreshStructureResult =
+  | { kind: 'matched'; metadata: RefreshStructure }
+  | { kind: 'unsupported' | 'invalid'; message: string }
+  | { kind: 'unrecognized' };
+
+function decodeSegment(value: string): Uint8Array | undefined {
+  if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(value) || value.length % 4 === 1) return;
+  try {
+    const bytes = base64url.decode(value);
+    return base64url.encode(bytes) === value ? bytes : undefined;
+  } catch { return; }
+}
+
+function hex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
+  return [...bytes].map(value => value.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
+}
+
+// .NET GUIDs store the first 4/2/2-byte groups little-endian, not all 16 bytes reversed.
+function dotNetGuid(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
+  const view = new DataView(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);
+  return [
+    view.getUint32(0, true).toString(16).padStart(8, '0'),
+    view.getUint16(4, true).toString(16).padStart(4, '0'),
+    view.getUint16(6, true).toString(16).padStart(4, '0'),
+    hex(bytes.subarray(8, 10)),
+    hex(bytes.subarray(10, 16)),
+  ].join('-');
+}
+
+// Experimental sample layout, NOT a Microsoft-guaranteed RT/PRT protocol:
+// https://insinuator.net/2026/10/token-theft-in-microsoft-entra-id-part-3-of-4-token-protection/
+export function parseRefreshStructure(input: string): RefreshStructureResult {
+  if (new TextEncoder().encode(input).length > MAX_INPUT_BYTES) {
+    return { kind: 'invalid', message: '输入超过 64 KiB，请缩短输入。' };
+  }
+  const token = input.trim().replace(/^Bearer(?:\s+|$)/i, '').trim();
+  if (!token) return { kind: 'unrecognized' };
+  if (/\s/.test(token)) return { kind: 'invalid', message: 'Token 内部含空白，请仅输入单个完整 Token。' };
+  const parts = token.split('.');
+  if (parts.length === 1 || !/^\d+$/.test(parts[0])) return { kind: 'unrecognized' };
+  if (parts.length !== 3) return { kind: 'invalid', message: '候选 RT/PRT 应包含版本、描述段和不透明载荷三段；已停止解释。' };
+  if (parts[0] !== '1') return { kind: 'unsupported', message: '未支持此版本；只按研究样例解释版本 1，已停止解释。' };
+  const descriptor = decodeSegment(parts[1]);
+  if (!descriptor) return { kind: 'invalid', message: '描述段不是规范 Base64URL 编码；已停止解释。' };
+  if (descriptor.length !== 40) return { kind: 'unsupported', message: '描述段长度不为 40 字节，与研究样例布局不符；已停止解释。' };
+  const opaque = decodeSegment(parts[2]);
+  if (!opaque) return { kind: 'invalid', message: '不透明载荷为空或不是规范 Base64URL 编码；已停止解释。' };
+  return {
+    kind: 'matched',
+    metadata: {
+      version: '1', descriptorBytes: 40,
+      prefixHex: hex(descriptor.subarray(0, 3)),
+      tenantId: dotNetGuid(descriptor.subarray(3, 19)),
+      clientId: dotNetGuid(descriptor.subarray(19, 35)),
+      trailerHex: hex(descriptor.subarray(35, 40)),
+      opaqueBytes: opaque.length,
+    },
+  };
+}

+import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
+import { base64url } from 'jose';
+import { brokerId, descriptor, opaqueText, refreshFixture, tenantId } from '../fixtures/refresh-structure';

+test('Refresh displays only experimental public metadata without network, claims, signature or copies', async ({ page }) => {
+  const external: string[] = [];
+  const errors: string[] = [];
+  page.on('pageerror', error => errors.push(error.message));
+  await page.route('**/*', route => {
+    if (new URL(route.request().url()).hostname === '127.0.0.1') return route.continue();
+    external.push(route.request().url());
+    return route.abort();
+  });
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  await page.context().setOffline(true);
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(`Bearer ${refreshFixture()}`);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toHaveText('公开描述段已解码 · 实验性 / 未验证');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toBeVisible();
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata-fields tr')).toHaveCount(8);
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-field="tenant_id"] code')).toHaveText(tenantId);
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-field="client_id"] code')).toHaveText(brokerId);
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-field="client_name"] code')).toHaveText('Microsoft Authentication Broker');
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-field="prefix_hex"] code')).toHaveText('010100');
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-field="trailer_hex"] code')).toHaveText('0000000000');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toContainText('未解密');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).not.toContainText(opaqueText);
+  await expect(page.locator('#details')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#signature-status')).toHaveText('签名未验证');
+  await expect(page.locator('#copy-header')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#copy-payload')).toBeDisabled();
+  await expect(page.locator('#header-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#token-colors .token-segment')).toHaveCount(0);
+  await page.waitForTimeout(400);
+  expect(external).toEqual([]);
+  expect(errors).toEqual([]);
+  expect(await page.evaluate(() => [localStorage.length, sessionStorage.length])).toEqual([0, 0]);
+  await page.screenshot({ path: 'test-results/refresh-structure-desktop.png', fullPage: true });
+  await page.setViewportSize({ width: 320, height: 740 });
+  expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= innerWidth)).toBe(true);
+  await page.screenshot({ path: 'test-results/refresh-structure-mobile.png', fullPage: true });
+});
+
+test('unsupported layouts and malformed input remove old metadata instead of guessing', async ({ page }) => {
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  for (const [value, message] of [
+    [refreshFixture('2'), '未支持此版本'],
+    [refreshFixture('1', descriptor.subarray(0, 39)), '长度不为 40'],
+    ['1.@@@.AA', '不是规范 Base64URL'],
+    ['x'.repeat(65537), '64 KiB'],
+  ]) {
+    await page.locator('#token').fill(refreshFixture());
+    await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toBeVisible();
+    await page.locator('#token').fill(value);
+    await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toBeHidden();
+    await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata-fields tr')).toHaveCount(0);
+    await expect(page.locator('#warnings')).toContainText(message);
+    await expect(page.locator('#refresh-details')).not.toContainText(tenantId);
+  }
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(refreshFixture());
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toBeVisible();
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('opaque-example');
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('Opaque / Unknown');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata-fields tr')).toHaveCount(0);
+});
+
+test('clear, tab switches and pending parsing never restore or leak descriptor fields', async ({ page }) => {
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(refreshFixture());
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toBeVisible();
+  await page.locator('#clear').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toBeHidden();
+  await page.evaluate(value => {
+    const input = document.getElementById('token') as HTMLTextAreaElement;
+    input.value = value;
+    input.dispatchEvent(new Event('input'));
+    document.getElementById('tab-id')!.click();
+  }, refreshFixture());
+  await page.waitForTimeout(400);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toHaveText('等待输入');
+  await expect(page.locator('#token')).toHaveValue('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(refreshFixture());
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toBeVisible();
+  await page.locator('#tab-access').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata-fields')).toHaveText('');
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).not.toContainText(tenantId);
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toBeHidden();
+});
+
+test('unknown clients stay unknown and JWT or HTML input is not interpreted as descriptor metadata', async ({ page }) => {
+  await page.goto('/');
+  await page.locator('#tab-refresh').click();
+  const unknown = descriptor.slice(); unknown.fill(0, 19, 35);
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(refreshFixture('1', unknown));
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-field="client_name"] code')).toContainText('未收录');
+  await expect(page.locator('[data-field="client_id"] code')).toHaveText('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000');
+  const cookie = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"HS256","kdf_ver":2}')}.${base64url.encode(JSON.stringify({ refresh_token: refreshFixture() }))}.AA`;
+  await page.locator('#token').fill(cookie);
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('格式：JWT');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata')).toBeHidden();
+  await expect(page.locator('#payload-json')).not.toContainText(tenantId);
+  await page.locator('#token').fill('1.<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>.AA');
+  await expect(page.locator('#status')).toContainText('未能解析');
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-details img')).toHaveCount(0);
+  await expect(page.locator('#refresh-metadata-fields tr')).toHaveCount(0);
+});

+import { base64url } from 'jose';
+
+// Synthetic envelope: known GUID bytes, arbitrary prefix/trailer and a NON-secret fake payload.
+// IDs are fixed public sample values; no real RT/PRT or key is used.
+export const tenantId = '1ce8c5a4-782d-4bef-b3a5-6e7e51a795f9';
+export const brokerId = '38aa3b87-a06d-4817-b275-7a316988d93b';
+export const descriptor = Uint8Array.from(
+  '010100a4c5e81c2d78ef4bb3a56e7e51a795f9873baa386da01748b2757a316988d93b0000000000'.match(/../g)!,
+  value => parseInt(value, 16),
+);
+export const opaqueText = 'SYNTHETIC_OPAQUE_NOT_REAL';
+export function refreshFixture(version = '1', bytes = descriptor): string {
+  return `${version}.${base64url.encode(bytes)}.${base64url.encode(opaqueText)}`;
+}

+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
+import { base64url } from 'jose';
+import { MAX_INPUT_BYTES, inspectToken } from '../src/core';
+import { modes } from '../src/modes';
+import { parseRefreshStructure } from '../src/refresh-structure';
+import { brokerId, descriptor, opaqueText, refreshFixture, tenantId } from './fixtures/refresh-structure';
+
+describe('experimental RT/PRT public descriptor', () => {
+  it('decodes exact offsets and .NET GUID byte order, not the opaque payload', () => {
+    const result = parseRefreshStructure(refreshFixture());
+    expect(result).toEqual({
+      kind: 'matched',
+      metadata: {
+        version: '1', descriptorBytes: 40, prefixHex: '010100', tenantId, clientId: brokerId,
+        trailerHex: '0000000000', opaqueBytes: new TextEncoder().encode(opaqueText).length,
+      },
+    });
+    expect(JSON.stringify(result)).not.toContain(opaqueText);
+    expect(JSON.stringify(result)).not.toContain(base64url.encode(opaqueText));
+  });
+
+  it('supports trimmed inputs and Bearer without modifying or uploading them', () => {
+    const value = refreshFixture();
+    expect(parseRefreshStructure(` \n bEaReR ${value} \r\n`)).toEqual(parseRefreshStructure(value));
+  });
+
+  it.each(['0', '2', '01', '999'])('does not infer fields for unsupported version %s', version => {
+    expect(parseRefreshStructure(refreshFixture(version))).toMatchObject({ kind: 'unsupported' });
+    expect(modes.refresh.inspect(refreshFixture(version)).refreshStructure).toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it.each([1, 16, 39, 41, 48])('stops interpretation for %s descriptor bytes', length => {
+    const result = parseRefreshStructure(refreshFixture('1', new Uint8Array(length)));
+    expect(result).toMatchObject({ kind: 'unsupported' });
+    expect('metadata' in result).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  const segment = base64url.encode(descriptor);
+  it.each([
+    ['empty descriptor', '1..AA'], ['illegal alphabet', '1.@@@.AA'],
+    ['padded descriptor', `1.${segment}==.AA`], ['noncanonical bits', `1.${segment.slice(0, -1)}B.AA`],
+    ['empty payload', `1.${segment}.`], ['illegal payload', `1.${segment}.??`],
+    ['noncanonical payload', `1.${segment}.AB`], ['missing segment', `1.${segment}`],
+    ['extra segment', `1.${segment}.AA.AA`], ['internal space', `1.${segment}.A A`],
+  ])('rejects %s without exposing partial IDs', (_label, value) => {
+    const parsed = parseRefreshStructure(value);
+    expect(parsed).toMatchObject({ kind: 'invalid' });
+    const result = modes.refresh.inspect(value);
+    expect(result.status).toBe('error');
+    expect(result.refreshStructure).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(result.fields).toEqual([]);
+  });
+
+  it('enforces the UTF-8 byte limit before decoding', () => {
+    for (const value of ['x'.repeat(MAX_INPUT_BYTES + 1), 'é'.repeat(MAX_INPUT_BYTES / 2 + 1)]) {
+      expect(parseRefreshStructure(value)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'invalid', message: expect.stringContaining('64 KiB') });
+    }
+  });
+
+  it.each(['', 'Bearer ', 'opaque-example', 'abc.def.ghi'])('leaves other inputs to existing classification: %s', value => {
+    expect(parseRefreshStructure(value)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'unrecognized' });
+    expect(modes.refresh.inspect(value).refreshStructure).toBeUndefined();
+  });
+
+  it('does not interpret an outer JWT/PRT Cookie or change Access/ID decoding', () => {
+    const cookie = `${base64url.encode('{"alg":"HS256","kdf_ver":2}')}.${base64url.encode(JSON.stringify({ refresh_token: refreshFixture() }))}.AA`;
+    expect(parseRefreshStructure(cookie)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'unrecognized' });
+    expect(modes.refresh.inspect(cookie).refreshStructure).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(modes.refresh.inspect(cookie).claims).toBeUndefined();
+    for (const purpose of ['access', 'id'] as const) {
+      expect(modes[purpose].inspect(refreshFixture())).toEqual(inspectToken(refreshFixture()));
+    }
+  });
+
+  it('keeps matched structure separate from verified identity, claims and lifetime', () => {
+    const result = modes.refresh.inspect(refreshFixture());
+    expect(result.format).toBe('Entra RT / PRT（候选结构）');
+    expect(result.status).toBe('opaque');
+    expect(result.verification).toBe('not-performed');
+    expect(result.refreshStructure?.tenantId).toBe(tenantId);
+    expect(result.header).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(result.claims).toBeUndefined();
+    expect(result.fields).toEqual([]);
+    expect(result.times).toEqual({});
+    expect(result.warnings.join(' ')).toMatch(/实验性/);
+    expect(result.warnings.join(' ')).toMatch(/不解密/);
+    expect(result.warnings.join(' ')).toMatch(/不确认/);
+  });
+});

 - 单个 JWT 解码，Header / Payload / 中文解释；输入支持 Bearer 前缀，最大 64 KiB UTF-8。
 - Entra ID v1/v2 字段含义解释与取值匹配（amr 认证方法、appidacr/azpacr 客户端认证方式、ver 与发行方格式、消费者租户 GUID、scp/roles 数量、xms_cc cp1、groups overage 等）；发行方识别是未验证的域名线索，不是身份认证。
-- scp/roles 逐值渲染为权限徽章，常见权限带中文说明，租户级/可提权等高危权限标红；azp/appid 与 aud 匹配已知第一方应用名与 API 名，FOCI/BroCi 客户端一律标红（悬停可见换票原因）。匹配是静态知识表，不代表租户实际授权，也不是身份认证。
+- 权限解释先按 `aud` 选择资源字典。首版仅收录 Microsoft Graph 的 GUID 与 `https://graph.microsoft.com`（可带末尾 `/`）精确别名；缺失、多值、类型异常、其他资源或仅匹配到应用名时，保留原始 JSON，不套用 Graph 描述或高危规则。
+- Access 入口的 `scp` 按委托范围解释；`roles` 仅在声明 `idtyp=app` 且完全没有 `scp` 时，按“应用权限线索（未验证）”选择应用权限字典。其余 `roles` 视为应用角色/未确定上下文，保留原值；`idtyp=app` 与 `scp` 同时出现时提示线索冲突，两类值都不套表。ID 入口不套用 API 权限字典。`wids` 单独标为目录角色 RoleTemplateID，保留 GUID，不与 API 权限混用。
+- Graph 委托和应用权限使用不同描述，仅对该类型下已收录的精确值生成说明及风险标记；未收录值不按名称前缀猜测。中文说明来自微软 MIT 许可的公开权限元数据，筛选范围限于原有知识表中的名称；它是本地静态快照，不是完整、实时的权限目录，也不代表租户实际授权。
+- azp/appid 与 aud 仍匹配已知第一方应用名与 API 名，FOCI/BroCi 客户端维持原有标记；这些标记与权限资源字典分开处理，不是身份认证。
 - 紧凑 JWE 公开 Header 展示，不解密、不验证加密参数或完整性。
 - 不透明或其他未知 Token 提示限制。Entra Refresh Token 的加密内容只能由 Microsoft identity platform 读取；Refresh 页的公开描述段解析不是密文解密。
 - 使用本机时钟展示 UTC 时间与声明时间诊断；缺失 exp 不代表永不过期。
@@ -59,7 +62,8 @@ npm run preview
 
 - `src/core.ts`：格式分类、jose 解码、时间诊断、统一 `InspectionResult`。
 - `src/providers.ts`：普通模块注册的厂商解释器，目前仅 Entra ID；提供全部官方声明的中文解释与常见取值匹配。
-- `src/permissions.ts`：权限值、第一方客户端 ID、受众静态知识表与匹配规则（来源含 GraphPreConsentExplorer 数据集），提供高危标记。
+- `src/permissions.ts`：aud 与权限类型上下文、精确权限匹配、第一方客户端和受众名称匹配。
+- `src/graph-permissions.ts`：按委托/应用类型分开的 Graph 中文权限描述快照；来源与 MIT 许可见 `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt`，运行时不联网更新。
 - `src/main.ts`：用途切换、公共输入事件及解析调度；切换即清空状态。
 - `src/modes/`：Access、ID、Refresh 的独立入口定义；Access / ID 共享底层解析，Refresh 仅传递格式与实验性公开元数据，不传递解码声明。
 - `src/refresh-structure.ts`：实验性描述段布局检查、Base64URL 解码与 .NET GUID 转换；无密文解密或联网功能。

+import { GRAPH_PERMISSIONS } from './graph-permissions';

+import type { TokenPurpose } from './modes/types';

+export { GRAPH_PERMISSIONS } from './graph-permissions';

+

+export interface PermissionInfo { description: string; critical?: boolean }

+export interface ClientAppInfo { name: string; note?: string; foci?: boolean; broci?: boolean; critical?: boolean }

+

+function isCriticalPermission(name: string): boolean {

+  return name === 'full_access_as_app' ||

+    name === 'Sites.FullControl.All' ||

+    name === 'User.ReadWrite.All' ||

+    name.startsWith('Directory.Read') ||

+    name.startsWith('Directory.Write') ||

+    name.startsWith('Directory.Access') ||

+    name.startsWith('RoleManagement.ReadWrite') ||

+    name === 'RoleManagement.Read.Directory' ||

+    name.startsWith('Application.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('AppRoleAssignment.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('DelegatedPermissionGrant.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('UserAuthenticationMethod.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('IdentityProvider.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('Policy.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('Privileged') ||

+    name.startsWith('AccessReview.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('EntitlementManagement.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('ConsentRequest.ReadApprove') ||

+    name.startsWith('ConsentRequest.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('Mail.ReadWrite.All') ||

+    name.startsWith('DeviceManagementConfiguration.ReadWrite') ||

+    name.startsWith('SecurityEvents.ReadWrite');

+}

+

+export type PermissionClaim = 'scp' | 'roles' | 'wids';

+export type PermissionKind = 'delegated' | 'application' | 'app-role' | 'directory-role';

+export interface PermissionContext {

+  resource?: 'graph';

+  kind: PermissionKind;

+  label: string;

+  notice: string;

+}

+

+export function permissionContext(claims: Record<string, unknown>, purpose: TokenPurpose, claim: PermissionClaim): PermissionContext {

+  // Only exact single-resource aliases select a dictionary; display-name matching is not enough.

+  const aud = typeof claims.aud === 'string' ? claims.aud : undefined;

+  const graph = aud !== undefined && (

+    aud.toLowerCase() === '00000003-0000-0000-c000-000000000000' ||

+    aud === 'https://graph.microsoft.com' || aud === 'https://graph.microsoft.com/'

+  );

+  const hasScopes = Object.hasOwn(claims, 'scp');

+  const conflicting = claims.idtyp === 'app' && hasScopes;

+  const resource = purpose === 'access' && graph && !conflicting ? 'graph' as const : undefined;

+  const appOnly = purpose === 'access' && claims.idtyp === 'app' && !hasScopes;

+  const kind: PermissionKind = claim === 'scp' ? 'delegated' : claim === 'wids' ? 'directory-role' : appOnly ? 'application' : 'app-role';

+  const label = {

+    delegated: '委托权限范围（scp）',

+    application: '应用权限（idtyp=app 线索，未验证）',

+    'app-role': '应用角色（roles，未判定为应用权限）',

+    'directory-role': '目录角色（wids / RoleTemplateID）',

+  }[kind];

+  let notice: string;

+  if (kind === 'directory-role') notice = '保留目录角色原值；目录角色不是 API 的 scope 或应用权限，不套用 Graph 权限字典。';

+  else if (conflicting) notice = 'idtyp=app 与 scp 同时出现，主体线索冲突；保留原值，不套用 Graph 权限字典。';

+  else if (kind === 'app-role') notice = 'roles 也可表示用户在目标应用的角色；缺少明确的应用权限上下文，保留原值，不套用 Graph 应用权限字典。';

+  else if (purpose !== 'access') notice = '当前不是 Access Token 入口，不将声明值解释为 API 权限；保留原值。';

+  else if (!resource) notice = '当前 aud 未匹配已收录的单一资源权限字典；保留原值，不套用 Microsoft Graph 说明或推断高危。';

+  else notice = `按 aud 声明匹配 Microsoft Graph ${kind === 'delegated' ? '委托' : '应用'}权限字典；仅静态解释，不验证实际授权或可用性。`;

+  return { resource, kind, label, notice };

+}

+

+export function permissionInfo(value: string, context: PermissionContext): PermissionInfo | undefined {

+  if (context.resource !== 'graph' || (context.kind !== 'delegated' && context.kind !== 'application')) return;

+  if (!Object.hasOwn(GRAPH_PERMISSIONS, value)) return;

+  const known = GRAPH_PERMISSIONS[value][context.kind];

+  if (!known) return;

+  return { ...known, critical: isCriticalPermission(value) };

+}

+

+

+// FOCI and BroCi first-party clients are always highlighted as critical: their refresh

+// tokens can be redeemed across sibling clients, which widens token-theft blast radius.

+export function clientApp(id: string): ClientAppInfo | undefined {

+  const app = CLIENT_APPS[id];

+  if (!app) return;

+  return app.critical || app.foci || app.broci ? { ...app, critical: true } : app;

+}

+

+export const AUDIENCES: Record<string, string> = {

+  '00000003-0000-0000-c000-000000000000': 'Microsoft Graph',

+  '00000002-0000-0000-c000-000000000000': 'Azure AD Graph（已弃用）',

+  '797f4846-ba00-4fd7-ba43-dac1f8f63013': 'Azure 资源管理器 (ARM)',

+  '00000003-0000-0ff1-ce00-000000000000': 'SharePoint / OneDrive',

+  '00000009-0000-0000-c000-000000000000': 'Power BI 服务',

+  '00000007-0000-0000-c000-000000000000': 'Dynamics CRM',

+  'cc15fd57-2c6c-4117-a88c-83b1d56b4bb7': 'Microsoft Teams',

+  'c5393580-f805-4401-95e8-94b7a6ef2fc2': 'Office 365 管理 API',

+  'https://management.core.windows.net/': 'Azure 资源管理器（经典端点）',

+  'https://management.azure.com/': 'Azure 资源管理器',

+  'https://graph.microsoft.com': 'Microsoft Graph',

+  'https://outlook.office365.com/': 'Exchange Online',

+  'https://vault.azure.net': 'Azure Key Vault',

+  'https://storage.azure.com/': 'Azure Storage 数据面',

+};

+

+export function splitScopes(value: unknown): string[] {

+  return typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() ? value.trim().split(/\s+/) : [];

+}

+

+export function roleValues(value: unknown): string[] {

+  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === 'string') : [];

+}

+

+export function audienceNames(value: unknown): string[] {

+  const values = typeof value === 'string' ? [value]

+    : Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === 'string')

+    : [];

+  return values.map(item => {

+    const id = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(item) ? item.toLowerCase() : item;

+    if (Object.hasOwn(AUDIENCES, id)) return AUDIENCES[id];

+    // An audience can also be a known application ID (e.g. an ID Token's client).

+    // A name match does not confirm token purpose, trust, or actual authorization.

+    if (Object.hasOwn(CLIENT_APPS, id)) return CLIENT_APPS[id].name;

+    return undefined;

+  }).filter((name): name is string => typeof name === 'string');

+}

+  await page.goto('/');
